default_model: opus
model_tier_rationale: changes the write-locked merge commit path (P0 data-safety blast radius), invents a new core result type plus a public GraphQL field, and breaks the GitMerge trait signature across core, CLI and server.
design: run
---

# Unwedge sync when a collision loser has no frontmatter

**Priority:** P1 · **Roadmap:** deferred-ledger tail (assessment 2026-09-06) · **Depends on:** none

## Overview

### Problem Statement

An add-add collision whose LOSING content carries no YAML frontmatter wedges sync permanently. `fold_one_loser` calls `parser::rewrite_id_field` (`ddb-core/src/git_ops/merge.rs:192`), which parses the loser through `find_frontmatter` and fails with `Parse("no frontmatter opening ---")` (`ddb-core/src/parser/mod.rs:180-184`, `:728`). The error propagates with `?` and aborts `commit_merge` before its single `create_commit`, so nothing lands — not the winner, not any earlier loser. The next sync recomputes the identical merge and fails identically: no `--skip`, no `--force`, no documented recovery. Before PRD 00167 the winner commit landed and sync converged (lossily); today it never converges. Provenance: `dev/local/audit-results/refactor-assessment-2026-09-06.md` (unowned deferred tail, "00167 frontmatter-less collision-loser sync wedge (no recovery path)") and `dev/local/autopilot/deferred/202607161128-deferred.json` (00167 cycle 2, topic `malformed-loser-wedges-sync`, HIGH, deferred at the rework cap with no PRD home).

The abort itself was the correct 00167 ruling for the general case: skipping a loser DELETES its content, because a loser only survives by being reassigned. But this specific case has a third option that is neither abort nor data loss, and it is coherent precisely because the failing content has no frontmatter `id` to leave stale: fold the loser at its derived content-id path with its bytes unchanged.

**Decision (maintainer, 2026-09-06, settled — do not re-open):** implement the byte-unchanged fold. It applies when, and only when, the losing content has no parseable frontmatter block; a parse failure for any other reason (well-formed delimiters, unparseable YAML inside) still aborts the whole merge exactly as it does today.

### Target Users

Operators syncing two or more ddb devices where a `ddb/{id}.md` was hand-authored (outside ddb's own write paths, which always emit frontmatter) on two devices under the same id. Their sync is currently dead with no operator recourse. Also downstream apps (jink) whose sync call never succeeds again once this state is reached.

### Success Metrics

- A two-device add-add collision with a frontmatter-less loser converges in ONE sync round (today: never).
- Zero bytes lost: the loser's file content is byte-identical before and after the merge.
- The event is machine-readable, not log-only: it appears on `SyncReport` and on every transport that renders `SyncReport`.
- No behavior change for losers WITH frontmatter (id-rewritten as today) or for other parse failures (still abort atomically).

## Functional Decomposition

### Capability: Frontmatter-less loser fold

Decide per loser whether its content can carry a rewritten id, and fold it into the merge tree either way.

#### Feature: Classify loser content by frontmatter presence
- **Description**: One pure predicate decides whether a loser's bytes contain a usable frontmatter block.
- **Inputs**: `&str` loser content.
- **Outputs**: `bool`.
- **Behavior**: `parser::has_frontmatter(content)` is true iff `find_frontmatter` locates BOTH the opening and the closing `---` line (`ddb-core/src/parser/mod.rs:180-192`). Missing opening, missing closing, or empty content are all false. Nothing else is inspected — a well-formed block whose YAML is invalid is TRUE here, so it still reaches `rewrite_id_field` and still aborts.

#### Feature: Fold a frontmatter-less loser byte-unchanged
- **Description**: When the predicate is false, the loser lands at its derived path with its original bytes instead of aborting the merge.
- **Inputs**: `CollisionLoser`, the in-memory merge index, the winner commit, the taken-id set.
- **Outputs**: an index entry at the derived path; one kept-verbatim record.
- **Behavior**: `id_minting::derive_content_id(old_id, losing_blob_oid, exists)` (`ddb-core/src/id_minting.rs:71`) still supplies the new id and `doogat_path` the new path, so both devices derive the same destination. `rewrite_id_field` is skipped, the original bytes are blobbed, and inbound-link rewriting still runs unchanged. Everything stays inside the existing `with_write_lock` and the single `create_commit`.

### Capability: Report the verbatim fold

Make the event visible to operators and downstream apps, not only to `tracing`.

#### Feature: Merge outcome carries kept-verbatim records
- **Description**: `commit_merge` returns the commit hash plus what it kept verbatim.
- **Inputs**: the losers batch.
- **Outputs**: `MergeCommitOutcome { commit: CommitHash, losers_kept_verbatim: Vec<CollisionLoserKeptVerbatim> }`.
- **Behavior**: replaces the bare `Result<CommitHash>` on the inherent method, the `GitMerge` trait (`ddb-core/src/traits.rs:408-415`) and the delegating impl (`ddb-core/src/git_ops/mod.rs:714-723`). Records are emitted only after `create_commit` succeeds, so an aborted merge reports nothing.

#### Feature: SyncReport field and transport renders
- **Description**: the records reach the CLI and GraphQL sync surfaces.
- **Inputs**: `MergeCommitOutcome` at `ddb-core/src/sync_manager/mod.rs:372`.
- **Outputs**: `SyncReport.collision_losers_kept_verbatim: Vec<CollisionLoserKeptVerbatim>`.
- **Behavior**: the new field lands in the core type first (`ddb-core/src/types/doogat.rs:218-232`), initialized empty at the report's single construction site (`ddb-core/src/sync_manager/mod.rs:283-291`). These losers are still counted by `collisions_reassigned` — they ARE reassigned — and additionally listed here. CLI and GraphQL each render it; no other transport exposes sync (see Transport Impact).

## Structural Decomposition

### Repository Structure

```
ddb-core/src/
├── parser/mod.rs              # Maps to: Classify loser content by frontmatter presence
├── git_ops/merge.rs           # Maps to: Fold a frontmatter-less loser byte-unchanged
├── traits.rs, git_ops/mod.rs  # Maps to: Merge outcome carries kept-verbatim records
├── types/doogat.rs            # Maps to: SyncReport field and transport renders
└── sync_manager/mod.rs        # Maps to: SyncReport field and transport renders
ddb-cli/src/commands/sync.rs   # Maps to: SyncReport field and transport renders
ddb-server/src/schema/mutations/operations.rs  # Maps to: SyncReport field and transport renders
```

### Module: parser
- **Maps to capability**: Frontmatter-less loser fold
- **Responsibility**: pure content classification; no git, no id knowledge.
- **Exports**:
  - `has_frontmatter(&str) -> bool` - true iff both frontmatter delimiters are present

### Module: git_ops::merge
- **Maps to capability**: Frontmatter-less loser fold; Report the verbatim fold
- **Responsibility**: build the merge tree, fold every loser, commit once under the write lock; owns the `GitMerge::commit_merge` declaration in `traits.rs` and its delegating impl in `git_ops/mod.rs`.
- **Exports**:
  - `commit_merge(...) -> Result<MergeCommitOutcome>` - the single merge-commit entry point, inherent and via `GitMerge`

### Module: types
- **Maps to capability**: Report the verbatim fold
- **Responsibility**: adapter-neutral shapes shared by every transport.
- **Exports**:
  - `CollisionLoserKeptVerbatim { old_id, old_path, new_path }` - one verbatim fold
  - `MergeCommitOutcome` - commit hash plus the verbatim folds
  - `SyncReport.collision_losers_kept_verbatim` - per-sync list

### Module: sync_manager
- **Maps to capability**: Report the verbatim fold
- **Responsibility**: map merge outcomes into the sync report; owns no fold policy.
- **Exports**:
  - `SyncManager::sync` - unchanged signature, richer report

### Module: ddb-cli::commands::sync
- **Maps to capability**: Report the verbatim fold
- **Responsibility**: render one `SyncReport` to stdout; holds no policy and re-derives nothing.
- **Exports**:
  - `sync()` - prints the verbatim-fold lines when the vector is non-empty

### Module: ddb-server::schema::mutations::operations
- **Maps to capability**: Report the verbatim fold
- **Responsibility**: serialize the same `SyncReport` into the GraphQL sync mutation result.
- **Exports**:
  - `collisionLosersKeptVerbatim` - GraphQL list mirroring the core vector field-for-field

## Dependency Graph

### Foundation Layer (Phase 0)
No dependencies - built first.

- **parser**: the `has_frontmatter` predicate
- **types**: `CollisionLoserKeptVerbatim`, `MergeCommitOutcome`, the `SyncReport` field

### Core Layer (Phase 1)
- **git_ops::merge**: Depends on [parser, types]
- **sync_manager**: Depends on [git_ops::merge, types]

### Integration Layer (Phase 2)
- **ddb-cli / ddb-server / docs**: Depends on [sync_manager, types]

## Implementation Phases

### Phase 0: Foundation
**Goal**: the predicate and the shapes exist, with nothing depending on them yet.

**Tasks**:
- [ ] Add `parser::has_frontmatter(&str) -> bool` beside `find_frontmatter`, true iff both delimiters are found (no deps) - Acceptance: `rg -q "fn has_frontmatter_rejects_content_with_no_opening_delimiter" ddb-core/src/parser` exits 0 (a cargo filter matching zero tests exits 0, so presence is asserted separately) and `cargo test -p ddb-core has_frontmatter` passes it plus `has_frontmatter_rejects_unterminated_block` and `has_frontmatter_accepts_well_formed_block`.
- [ ] Add `CollisionLoserKeptVerbatim` and `MergeCommitOutcome` to `types/doogat.rs`; add `collision_losers_kept_verbatim` to `SyncReport`, initialized empty at `sync_manager/mod.rs:283-291` (no deps) - Acceptance: `cargo build --workspace` succeeds and `rg -q "collision_losers_kept_verbatim" ddb-core/src/types/doogat.rs` exits 0.

**Exit Criteria**: workspace builds; the predicate is unit-tested; no merge behavior has changed yet.

### Phase 1: Core
**Goal**: a frontmatter-less loser converges instead of wedging, and every other loser behaves exactly as before.

**Tasks**:
- [ ] In `fold_one_loser`, skip `rewrite_id_field` when `!parser::has_frontmatter(&loser.content)`, blob the original bytes at the derived path, and return a kept-verbatim record; thread `MergeCommitOutcome` through `commit_merge`, `traits.rs:408-415`, `git_ops/mod.rs:714-723` and `sync_manager/mod.rs:372`; replace the atomic-abort doc comment at `git_ops/merge.rs:127-134` with the two-branch rule. Premise: that comment still states the unconditional abort; re-check it at execution, rewrite it wherever it now lives, and report the drift if it already reads otherwise (the fold itself is unconditional and always ships) (depends on: Phase 0) - Acceptance: `rg -q "MergeCommitOutcome" ddb-core/src/traits.rs` exits 0, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets` is clean, and `cargo test-ci` passes.
- [ ] Add `git_ops/tests.rs` test `frontmatterless_loser_lands_byte_identical_at_its_derived_path`: the merge commits, HEAD moves, the winner lands, and the loser's bytes at `doogat_path(derive_content_id(old_id, losing_blob_oid))` equal its input bytes exactly. Premise: `git_ops/tests.rs:2672` `commit_merge_commits_nothing_when_one_loser_in_the_batch_cannot_be_rewritten` currently asserts the opposite for this input and must be re-pointed at a YAML-invalid loser; re-check that test name and its frontmatter-less fixture at execution and skip-and-report if it has moved (depends on: Phase 1 fold) - Acceptance: `rg -q "fn frontmatterless_loser_lands_byte_identical_at_its_derived_path" ddb-core/src/git_ops/tests.rs` exits 0 and `cargo test -p ddb-core frontmatterless_loser_lands_byte_identical_at_its_derived_path` passes.
- [ ] Add the two control tests: `loser_with_frontmatter_is_still_id_rewritten` (bytes equal `rewrite_id_field(content, derived_id)`, not the input) and `loser_with_unparseable_yaml_still_aborts_the_whole_batch` (well-formed `---` delimiters, invalid YAML body: `commit_merge` errs, HEAD does not move, nothing lands) (depends on: Phase 1 fold) - Acceptance: `rg -c "fn loser_with_" ddb-core/src/git_ops/tests.rs` reports 2 and `cargo test -p ddb-core loser_with_` runs both and passes.
- [ ] Map `MergeCommitOutcome.losers_kept_verbatim` onto `SyncReport` in `create_conflict_commit`/`resolve_merge_conflicts`, keeping these losers counted in `collisions_reassigned`; add `sync_manager/tests.rs` test `sync_report_lists_every_verbatim_folded_collision_loser` (depends on: Phase 1 fold) - Acceptance: `rg -q "fn sync_report_lists_every_verbatim_folded_collision_loser" ddb-core/src/sync_manager/tests.rs` exits 0 and `cargo test -p ddb-core sync_report_lists_every_verbatim_folded_collision_loser` passes.
- [ ] Re-point the bundle-import expectations that assert this input FAILS the import. Premise: `ddb-core/src/bundle/tests.rs:299` and `:335` build `import_with_unresolvable_collision` from a frontmatter-less loser and expect `Err`; that fixture now converges. Re-check both at execution; if the fixture no longer has that shape, skip and report (depends on: Phase 1 fold) - Acceptance: `cargo test -p ddb-core bundle::tests` passes with the frontmatter-less import asserted to SUCCEED and report one verbatim fold.

**Exit Criteria**: `cargo test-ci` green; a frontmatter-less loser commits, a YAML-invalid loser still aborts, and the report names the fold.

### Phase 2: Integration
**Goal**: operators and downstream apps can see the fold, and the behavior is documented and demonstrated.

**Tasks**:
- [ ] Render the field on both sync transports: a CLI line `  collision losers kept verbatim: N` (guess) printed only when non-empty, then one `    {old_path} -> {new_path}` line each (`ddb-cli/src/commands/sync.rs:18-20` pattern); a GraphQL `collisionLosersKeptVerbatim` (guess) list of `{ oldId, oldPath, newPath }` objects on the sync mutation result (`ddb-server/src/schema/mutations/operations.rs:360-363` pattern) (depends on: Phase 1) - Acceptance: `cargo test-ci` passes and `rg -q "collisionLosersKeptVerbatim" ddb-server/src/schema/mutations/operations.rs` exits 0.
- [ ] Author e2e scenario `add_add_collision_with_frontmatterless_loser_converges_in_one_sync` in `tests/e2e/collision.rs` (module already registered at `tests/e2e/main.rs:11`): two nodes commit the same `ddb/{id}.md`, one side without any frontmatter; `ddb sync` on the resolving node exits 0, prints the verbatim line, both files survive, and the frontmatter-less bytes are unchanged on disk (depends on: Phase 2 renders) - Acceptance: `rg -q "fn add_add_collision_with_frontmatterless_loser_converges_in_one_sync" tests/e2e/collision.rs` exits 0 and `cargo build --workspace --tests` succeeds; execution is Tier 2 CI.
- [ ] Update `docs/src/technical/sync.md` (replace the atomic-abort paragraph at `:121` with the two-branch rule plus recovery semantics: what the operator sees, where the file lands, that it stays unindexable until frontmatter is added), note the branch under I1 in `docs/src/technical/invariants.md`, add CHANGELOG `Fixed` (the wedge) and `Changed` (`GitMerge::commit_merge` return type) entries, and record the walkthrough in `dev/local/walkthroughs/` via the showboat CLI (depends on: Phase 2 renders) - Acceptance: `rg -q "kept verbatim" docs/src/technical/sync.md CHANGELOG.md` exits 0 and the walkthrough file exists.

**Exit Criteria**: `cargo build`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets`, `cargo test-ci` all clean; docs, CHANGELOG and walkthrough describe the converging behavior.

## Test Strategy

**Validation:** per-task local gates are `cargo build`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets`, and `cargo test-ci`, including colocated unit regressions. Author required e2e/integration/conformance scenarios and executable walkthroughs; their heavy execution belongs to Tier 2 CI, not the per-task gate. Retain only CLAUDE.md's conditional e2e run for Claude-routed tasks deleting/replacing code paths. Walkthrough creation uses showboat; any verification uses `dev/bin/safe-showboat-verify`, never the project root.

### Critical Scenarios
- **Happy path**: add-add collision, loser has no frontmatter → merge commits once; winner keeps the contested id; loser's bytes land byte-identical at `derive_content_id(old_id, losing_blob_oid)`; `SyncReport` lists one verbatim fold; next sync is a no-op.
- **Happy path (unchanged)**: add-add collision, loser HAS frontmatter → id-rewritten exactly as today, absent from the verbatim list.
- **Edge case**: two losers in one batch, one frontmatter-less and one normal → both land in the SAME commit; the verbatim list holds only the first.
- **Edge case**: two devices resolve the same frontmatter-less collision in opposite merge directions → identical destination path on both, because the derivation is unchanged.
- **Error case**: loser with well-formed `---` delimiters but unparseable YAML → `commit_merge` still errs, HEAD does not move, nothing lands (the 00167 atomicity guarantee survives for every non-frontmatter-less failure).

## Risks

- **Weakening the 00167 atomic-abort ruling**: the fold is gated on one narrow, structurally-checked predicate, and the YAML-invalid control test pins that every other failure still aborts the whole batch. The predicate is evaluated in exactly one place.
- **Breaking `GitMerge::commit_merge`**: a public library signature change with one production caller (`sync_manager/mod.rs:372`) plus tests. Mitigated by a CHANGELOG `Changed` entry, matching 00167's own precedent for this method.
- **A frontmatter-less doogat is unindexable**: it lands in git but the indexer will skip it as malformed and report it through the I5 poison-file warning path. That is strictly better than the wedge, and the docs task states it as the operator's follow-up (add frontmatter, commit).
- **Not covered here**: the same ledger's `independent-resolution-test-can-false-green` HIGH (`ddb-core/src/sync_manager/tests.rs:1888`) is deliberately out of scope — the ledger calls that fix cheap, but `resolve_collision_with_push_order` returns `Vec<String>` filenames (`ddb-core/src/sync_manager/tests.rs:1800`) and must first surface the losing side's blob OID before the derived path can be pinned, so it is more than a one-assertion change and stays out of this PRD. It remains unowned.

## Transport Impact

- **CLI**: CHANGED. `ddb sync` gains the verbatim-fold lines (printed only when non-empty). Covered by the e2e scenario in `tests/e2e/collision.rs`.
- **GraphQL**: CHANGED. The sync mutation result gains `collisionLosersKeptVerbatim`; purely additive, no existing field changes shape.
- **REST**: no change. `ddb-server/src/rest.rs` registers only `/doogats`, `/doogats/{id}` and `/schema/apply` — there is no sync endpoint to render.
- **PgWire**: no change. The wire is SQL-only; sync is not a SQL verb.
- **FFI**: no change. `DoogatDriver` exposes no `sync` method and `import_bundle` already discards its `SyncReport` (a pre-existing gap tracked in the 00168 deferred ledger, not widened here).
- **NoSQL HTTP**: no change. `ddb-server/src/nosql_api.rs` serves read-only `GET /{id}`, `GET /` and `GET /{id}/backlinks`.
- **Cross-interface conformance plan**: sync is a two-transport capability (CLI + GraphQL). Conformance is the shared core assertion — both transports read the SAME `SyncReport.collision_losers_kept_verbatim` vector with no per-transport field subset — plus the e2e CLI scenario above and a GraphQL assertion in the same run that the mutation returns one entry with the identical `oldPath`/`newPath` the CLI printed. No transport re-derives the list.

## Fail-first replay (a3e2dbc runtime, isolated worktree)

- The new tests cannot compile against the old `CommitHash` return. As the
  card allows, the replay therefore used the candidate files plus a one-token
  shim in the baseline copy of `merge.rs`:
  `if true || has_frontmatter(..)`. That forces the pre-FT-8a
  always-rewrite branch, which is exactly the old fold decision.
- Result (`FT-8a-logs/baseline-replay-core.log`): **5 FAILED, 48 passed**.
- Every failure was the wedge (`could not be rewritten: parse: no
  frontmatter opening ---`), not a setup or compile error:
  - frontmatterless_loser_lands_byte_identical_at_its_derived_path
  - frontmatterless_loser_derives_the_same_path_in_either_merge_direction
  - mixed_loser_batch_lands_in_one_commit_and_lists_only_the_verbatim_fold
  - sync_report_lists_every_verbatim_folded_collision_loser
  - frontmatterless_bundle_loser_imports_and_reports_one_verbatim_fold
    (`bundle merge failed: ...`)
- These passed on the shim:
  - the controls `loser_with_frontmatter_is_still_id_rewritten` and
    `loser_with_unparseable_yaml_still_aborts_the_whole_batch`;
  - the re-pointed `commit_merge_commits_nothing_when_one_loser...`;
  - all re-pointed bundle failure-path tests;
  - the three FFI bundle tests.
- Replay limits:
  - The shim changes the runtime of the baseline copy by one token. It is a
    shape adapter, not the untouched a3e2dbc binary.
  - The re-pointed CLI e2e fixtures were not replayed against the pre-FT-7
    runtime (a739c29). Their FFI twins passed on baseline, which shows the
    YAML-invalid import still fails.

# Unwedge sync when a collision loser has no frontmatter

**Priority:** P1 · **Roadmap:** deferred-ledger tail (assessment 2026-09-06) · **Depends on:** none

## Overview

### Problem Statement

An add-add collision whose LOSING content carries no YAML frontmatter wedges sync permanently. `fold_one_loser` calls `parser::rewrite_id_field` (`ddb-core/src/git_ops/merge.rs:192`), which parses the loser through `find_frontmatter` and fails with `Parse("no frontmatter opening ---")` (`ddb-core/src/parser/mod.rs:180-184`, `:728`). The error propagates with `?` and aborts `commit_merge` before its single `create_commit`, so nothing lands — not the winner, not any earlier loser. The next sync recomputes the identical merge and fails identically: no `--skip`, no `--force`, no documented recovery. Before PRD 00167 the winner commit landed and sync converged (lossily); today it never converges. Provenance: `dev/local/audit-results/refactor-assessment-2026-09-06.md` (unowned deferred tail, "00167 frontmatter-less collision-loser sync wedge (no recovery path)") and `dev/local/autopilot/deferred/202607161128-deferred.json` (00167 cycle 2, topic `malformed-loser-wedges-sync`, HIGH, deferred at the rework cap with no PRD home).

The abort itself was the correct 00167 ruling for the general case: skipping a loser DELETES its content, because a loser only survives by being reassigned. But this specific case has a third option that is neither abort nor data loss, and it is coherent precisely because the failing content has no frontmatter `id` to leave stale: fold the loser at its derived content-id path with its bytes unchanged.

**Decision (maintainer, 2026-09-06, settled — do not re-open):** implement the byte-unchanged fold. It applies when, and only when, the losing content has no parseable frontmatter block; a parse failure for any other reason (well-formed delimiters, unparseable YAML inside) still aborts the whole merge exactly as it does today.

### Target Users

Operators syncing two or more ddb devices where a `ddb/{id}.md` was hand-authored (outside ddb's own write paths, which always emit frontmatter) on two devices under the same id. Their sync is currently dead with no operator recourse. Also downstream apps (jink) whose sync call never succeeds again once this state is reached.

### Success Metrics

- A two-device add-add collision with a frontmatter-less loser converges in ONE sync round (today: never).
- Zero bytes lost: the loser's file content is byte-identical before and after the merge.
- The event is machine-readable, not log-only: it appears on `SyncReport` and on every transport that renders `SyncReport`.
- No behavior change for losers WITH frontmatter (id-rewritten as today) or for other parse failures (still abort atomically).

## Functional Decomposition

### Capability: Frontmatter-less loser fold

Decide per loser whether its content can carry a rewritten id, and fold it into the merge tree either way.

#### Feature: Classify loser content by frontmatter presence
- **Description**: One pure predicate decides whether a loser's bytes contain a usable frontmatter block.
- **Inputs**: `&str` loser content.
- **Outputs**: `bool`.
- **Behavior**: `parser::has_frontmatter(content)` is true iff `find_frontmatter` locates BOTH the opening and the closing `---` line (`ddb-core/src/parser/mod.rs:180-192`). Missing opening, missing closing, or empty content are all false. Nothing else is inspected — a well-formed block whose YAML is invalid is TRUE here, so it still reaches `rewrite_id_field` and still aborts.

#### Feature: Fold a frontmatter-less loser byte-unchanged
- **Description**: When the predicate is false, the loser lands at its derived path with its original bytes instead of aborting the merge.
- **Inputs**: `CollisionLoser`, the in-memory merge index, the winner commit, the taken-id set.
- **Outputs**: an index entry at the derived path; one kept-verbatim record.
- **Behavior**: `id_minting::derive_content_id(old_id, losing_blob_oid, exists)` (`ddb-core/src/id_minting.rs:71`) still supplies the new id and `doogat_path` the new path, so both devices derive the same destination. `rewrite_id_field` is skipped, the original bytes are blobbed, and inbound-link rewriting still runs unchanged. Everything stays inside the existing `with_write_lock` and the single `create_commit`.

### Capability: Report the verbatim fold

Make the event visible to operators and downstream apps, not only to `tracing`.

#### Feature: Merge outcome carries kept-verbatim records
- **Description**: `commit_merge` returns the commit hash plus what it kept verbatim.
- **Inputs**: the losers batch.
- **Outputs**: `MergeCommitOutcome { commit: CommitHash, losers_kept_verbatim: Vec<CollisionLoserKeptVerbatim> }`.
- **Behavior**: replaces the bare `Result<CommitHash>` on the inherent method, the `GitMerge` trait (`ddb-core/src/traits.rs:408-415`) and the delegating impl (`ddb-core/src/git_ops/mod.rs:714-723`). Records are emitted only after `create_commit` succeeds, so an aborted merge reports nothing.

#### Feature: SyncReport field and transport renders
- **Description**: the records reach the CLI and GraphQL sync surfaces.
- **Inputs**: `MergeCommitOutcome` at `ddb-core/src/sync_manager/mod.rs:372`.
- **Outputs**: `SyncReport.collision_losers_kept_verbatim: Vec<CollisionLoserKeptVerbatim>`.
- **Behavior**: the new field lands in the core type first (`ddb-core/src/types/doogat.rs:218-232`), initialized empty at the report's single construction site (`ddb-core/src/sync_manager/mod.rs:283-291`). These losers are still counted by `collisions_reassigned` — they ARE reassigned — and additionally listed here. CLI and GraphQL each render it; no other transport exposes sync (see Transport Impact).

## Structural Decomposition

### Repository Structure

```
ddb-core/src/
├── parser/mod.rs              # Maps to: Classify loser content by frontmatter presence
├── git_ops/merge.rs           # Maps to: Fold a frontmatter-less loser byte-unchanged
├── traits.rs, git_ops/mod.rs  # Maps to: Merge outcome carries kept-verbatim records
├── types/doogat.rs            # Maps to: SyncReport field and transport renders
└── sync_manager/mod.rs        # Maps to: SyncReport field and transport renders
ddb-cli/src/commands/sync.rs   # Maps to: SyncReport field and transport renders
ddb-server/src/schema/mutations/operations.rs  # Maps to: SyncReport field and transport renders
```

### Module: parser
- **Maps to capability**: Frontmatter-less loser fold
- **Responsibility**: pure content classification; no git, no id knowledge.
- **Exports**:
  - `has_frontmatter(&str) -> bool` - true iff both frontmatter delimiters are present

### Module: git_ops::merge
- **Maps to capability**: Frontmatter-less loser fold; Report the verbatim fold
- **Responsibility**: build the merge tree, fold every loser, commit once under the write lock; owns the `GitMerge::commit_merge` declaration in `traits.rs` and its delegating impl in `git_ops/mod.rs`.
- **Exports**:
  - `commit_merge(...) -> Result<MergeCommitOutcome>` - the single merge-commit entry point, inherent and via `GitMerge`

### Module: types
- **Maps to capability**: Report the verbatim fold
- **Responsibility**: adapter-neutral shapes shared by every transport.
- **Exports**:
  - `CollisionLoserKeptVerbatim { old_id, old_path, new_path }` - one verbatim fold
  - `MergeCommitOutcome` - commit hash plus the verbatim folds
  - `SyncReport.collision_losers_kept_verbatim` - per-sync list

### Module: sync_manager
- **Maps to capability**: Report the verbatim fold
- **Responsibility**: map merge outcomes into the sync report; owns no fold policy.
- **Exports**:
  - `SyncManager::sync` - unchanged signature, richer report

### Module: ddb-cli::commands::sync
- **Maps to capability**: Report the verbatim fold
- **Responsibility**: render one `SyncReport` to stdout; holds no policy and re-derives nothing.
- **Exports**:
  - `sync()` - prints the verbatim-fold lines when the vector is non-empty

### Module: ddb-server::schema::mutations::operations
- **Maps to capability**: Report the verbatim fold
- **Responsibility**: serialize the same `SyncReport` into the GraphQL sync mutation result.
- **Exports**:
  - `collisionLosersKeptVerbatim` - GraphQL list mirroring the core vector field-for-field

## Dependency Graph

### Foundation Layer (Phase 0)
No dependencies - built first.

- **parser**: the `has_frontmatter` predicate
- **types**: `CollisionLoserKeptVerbatim`, `MergeCommitOutcome`, the `SyncReport` field

### Core Layer (Phase 1)
- **git_ops::merge**: Depends on [parser, types]
- **sync_manager**: Depends on [git_ops::merge, types]

### Integration Layer (Phase 2)
- **ddb-cli / ddb-server / docs**: Depends on [sync_manager, types]

## Implementation Phases

### Phase 0: Foundation
**Goal**: the predicate and the shapes exist, with nothing depending on them yet.

**Tasks**:
- [ ] Add `parser::has_frontmatter(&str) -> bool` beside `find_frontmatter`, true iff both delimiters are found (no deps) - Acceptance: `rg -q "fn has_frontmatter_rejects_content_with_no_opening_delimiter" ddb-core/src/parser` exits 0 (a cargo filter matching zero tests exits 0, so presence is asserted separately) and `cargo test -p ddb-core has_frontmatter` passes it plus `has_frontmatter_rejects_unterminated_block` and `has_frontmatter_accepts_well_formed_block`.
- [ ] Add `CollisionLoserKeptVerbatim` and `MergeCommitOutcome` to `types/doogat.rs`; add `collision_losers_kept_verbatim` to `SyncReport`, initialized empty at `sync_manager/mod.rs:283-291` (no deps) - Acceptance: `cargo build --workspace` succeeds and `rg -q "collision_losers_kept_verbatim" ddb-core/src/types/doogat.rs` exits 0.

**Exit Criteria**: workspace builds; the predicate is unit-tested; no merge behavior has changed yet.

### Phase 1: Core
**Goal**: a frontmatter-less loser converges instead of wedging, and every other loser behaves exactly as before.

**Tasks**:
- [ ] In `fold_one_loser`, skip `rewrite_id_field` when `!parser::has_frontmatter(&loser.content)`, blob the original bytes at the derived path, and return a kept-verbatim record; thread `MergeCommitOutcome` through `commit_merge`, `traits.rs:408-415`, `git_ops/mod.rs:714-723` and `sync_manager/mod.rs:372`; replace the atomic-abort doc comment at `git_ops/merge.rs:127-134` with the two-branch rule. Premise: that comment still states the unconditional abort; re-check it at execution, rewrite it wherever it now lives, and report the drift if it already reads otherwise (the fold itself is unconditional and always ships) (depends on: Phase 0) - Acceptance: `rg -q "MergeCommitOutcome" ddb-core/src/traits.rs` exits 0, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets` is clean, and `cargo test-ci` passes.

Premise: `git_ops/tests.rs:2672` `commit_merge_commits_nothing_when_one_loser_in_the_batch_cannot_be_rewritten` currently asserts the opposite for this input and must be re-pointed at a YAML-invalid loser; re-check that test name and its frontmatter-less fixture at execution and skip-and-report if it has moved (depends on: Phase 1 fold) - Acceptance: `rg -q "fn frontmatterless_loser_lands_byte_identical_at_its_derived_path" ddb-core/src/git_ops/tests.rs` exits 0 and `cargo test -p ddb-core frontmatterless_loser_lands_byte_identical_at_its_derived_path` passes.
- [ ] Add the two control tests: `loser_with_frontmatter_is_still_id_rewritten` (bytes equal `rewrite_id_field(content, derived_id)`, not the input) and `loser_with_unparseable_yaml_still_aborts_the_whole_batch` (well-formed `---` delimiters, invalid YAML body: `commit_merge` errs, HEAD does not move, nothing lands) (depends on: Phase 1 fold) - Acceptance: `rg -c "fn loser_with_" ddb-core/src/git_ops/tests.rs` reports 2 and `cargo test -p ddb-core loser_with_` runs both and passes.
- [ ] Map `MergeCommitOutcome.losers_kept_verbatim` onto `SyncReport` in `create_conflict_commit`/`resolve_merge_conflicts`, keeping these losers counted in `collisions_reassigned`; add `sync_manager/tests.rs` test `sync_report_lists_every_verbatim_folded_collision_loser` (depends on: Phase 1 fold) - Acceptance: `rg -q "fn sync_report_lists_every_verbatim_folded_collision_loser" ddb-core/src/sync_manager/tests.rs` exits 0 and `cargo test -p ddb-core sync_report_lists_every_verbatim_folded_collision_loser` passes.
- [ ] Re-point the bundle-import expectations that assert this input FAILS the import. Premise: `ddb-core/src/bundle/tests.rs:299` and `:335` build `import_with_unresolvable_collision` from a frontmatter-less loser and expect `Err`; that fixture now converges. Re-check both at execution; if the fixture no longer has that shape, skip and report (depends on: Phase 1 fold) - Acceptance: `cargo test -p ddb-core bundle::tests` passes with the frontmatter-less import asserted to SUCCEED and report one verbatim fold.

**Exit Criteria**: `cargo test-ci` green; a frontmatter-less loser commits, a YAML-invalid loser still aborts, and the report names the fold.

### Phase 2: Integration
**Goal**: operators and downstream apps can see the fold, and the behavior is documented and demonstrated.

**Tasks**:
- [ ] Render the field on both sync transports: a CLI line `  collision losers kept verbatim: N` (guess) printed only when non-empty, then one `    {old_path} -> {new_path}` line each (`ddb-cli/src/commands/sync.rs:18-20` pattern); a GraphQL `collisionLosersKeptVerbatim` (guess) list of `{ oldId, oldPath, newPath }` objects on the sync mutation result (`ddb-server/src/schema/mutations/operations.rs:360-363` pattern) (depends on: Phase 1) - Acceptance: `cargo test-ci` passes and `rg -q "collisionLosersKeptVerbatim" ddb-server/src/schema/mutations/operations.rs` exits 0.
- [ ] Author e2e scenario `add_add_collision_with_frontmatterless_loser_converges_in_one_sync` in `tests/e2e/collision.rs` (module already registered at `tests/e2e/main.rs:11`): two nodes commit the same `ddb/{id}.md`, one side without any frontmatter; `ddb sync` on the resolving node exits 0, prints the verbatim line, both files survive, and the frontmatter-less bytes are unchanged on disk (depends on: Phase 2 renders) - Acceptance: `rg -q "fn add_add_collision_with_frontmatterless_loser_converges_in_one_sync" tests/e2e/collision.rs` exits 0 and `cargo build --workspace --tests` succeeds; execution is Tier 2 CI.
- [ ] Update `docs/src/technical/sync.md` (replace the atomic-abort paragraph at `:121` with the two-branch rule plus recovery semantics: what the operator sees, where the file lands, that it stays unindexable until frontmatter is added), note the branch under I1 in `docs/src/technical/invariants.md`, add CHANGELOG `Fixed` (the wedge) and `Changed` (`GitMerge::commit_merge` return type) entries, and record the walkthrough in `dev/local/walkthroughs/` via the showboat CLI (depends on: Phase 2 renders) - Acceptance: `rg -q "kept verbatim" docs/src/technical/sync.md CHANGELOG.md` exits 0 and the walkthrough file exists.

**Exit Criteria**: `cargo build`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets`, `cargo test-ci` all clean; docs, CHANGELOG and walkthrough describe the converging behavior.

## Test Strategy

**Validation:** per-task local gates are `cargo build`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets`, and `cargo test-ci`, including colocated unit regressions. Author required e2e/integration/conformance scenarios and executable walkthroughs; their heavy execution belongs to Tier 2 CI, not the per-task gate. Retain only CLAUDE.md's conditional e2e run for Claude-routed tasks deleting/replacing code paths. Walkthrough creation uses showboat; any verification uses `dev/bin/safe-showboat-verify`, never the project root.

### Critical Scenarios
- **Happy path**: add-add collision, loser has no frontmatter → merge commits once; winner keeps the contested id; loser's bytes land byte-identical at `derive_content_id(old_id, losing_blob_oid)`; `SyncReport` lists one verbatim fold; next sync is a no-op.
- **Happy path (unchanged)**: add-add collision, loser HAS frontmatter → id-rewritten exactly as today, absent from the verbatim list.
- **Edge case**: two losers in one batch, one frontmatter-less and one normal → both land in the SAME commit; the verbatim list holds only the first.
- **Edge case**: two devices resolve the same frontmatter-less collision in opposite merge directions → identical destination path on both, because the derivation is unchanged.
- **Error case**: loser with well-formed `---` delimiters but unparseable YAML → `commit_merge` still errs, HEAD does not move, nothing lands (the 00167 atomicity guarantee survives for every non-frontmatter-less failure).

## Risks

- **Weakening the 00167 atomic-abort ruling**: the fold is gated on one narrow, structurally-checked predicate, and the YAML-invalid control test pins that every other failure still aborts the whole batch. The predicate is evaluated in exactly one place.
- **Breaking `GitMerge::commit_merge`**: a public library signature change with one production caller (`sync_manager/mod.rs:372`) plus tests. Mitigated by a CHANGELOG `Changed` entry, matching 00167's own precedent for this method.
- **A frontmatter-less doogat is unindexable**: it lands in git but the indexer will skip it as malformed and report it through the I5 poison-file warning path. That is strictly better than the wedge, and the docs task states it as the operator's follow-up (add frontmatter, commit).
- **Not covered here**: the same ledger's `independent-resolution-test-can-false-green` HIGH (`ddb-core/src/sync_manager/tests.rs:1888`) is deliberately out of scope — the ledger calls that fix cheap, but `resolve_collision_with_push_order` returns `Vec<String>` filenames (`ddb-core/src/sync_manager/tests.rs:1800`) and must first surface the losing side's blob OID before the derived path can be pinned, so it is more than a one-assertion change and stays out of this PRD. It remains unowned.

## Transport Impact

- **CLI**: CHANGED. `ddb sync` gains the verbatim-fold lines (printed only when non-empty). Covered by the e2e scenario in `tests/e2e/collision.rs`.
- **GraphQL**: CHANGED. The sync mutation result gains `collisionLosersKeptVerbatim`; purely additive, no existing field changes shape.
- **REST**: no change. `ddb-server/src/rest.rs` registers only `/doogats`, `/doogats/{id}` and `/schema/apply` — there is no sync endpoint to render.
- **PgWire**: no change. The wire is SQL-only; sync is not a SQL verb.
- **FFI**: no change. `DoogatDriver` exposes no `sync` method and `import_bundle` already discards its `SyncReport` (a pre-existing gap tracked in the 00168 deferred ledger, not widened here).
- **NoSQL HTTP**: no change. `ddb-server/src/nosql_api.rs` serves read-only `GET /{id}`, `GET /` and `GET /{id}/backlinks`.
- **Cross-interface conformance plan**: sync is a two-transport capability (CLI + GraphQL). Conformance is the shared core assertion — both transports read the SAME `SyncReport.collision_losers_kept_verbatim` vector with no per-transport field subset — plus the e2e CLI scenario above and a GraphQL assertion in the same run that the mutation returns one entry with the identical `oldPath`/`newPath` the CLI printed. No transport re-derives the list.

+/// One frontmatter-less and one normal loser in the same call land in the
+/// SAME single merge commit; only the frontmatter-less one is reported.
+#[test]
+fn mixed_loser_batch_lands_in_one_commit_and_lists_only_the_verbatim_fold() {
+    let (_a, _b, _bare, repo_b, theirs_oid) = conflicted_add_add(FRONTMATTERLESS_LOSER, WINNER);
+    let verbatim = loser_at(
+        &repo_b,
+        CONTESTED_ID,
+        CONTESTED_PATH,
+        FRONTMATTERLESS_LOSER,
+        true,
+    );
+    let normal_content = "---\nid: 20260301130000\ntitle: Normal Loser\n---\nNormal body.\n";
+    let normal = loser_at(
+        &repo_b,
+        "20260301130000",
+        "ddb/20260301130000.md",
+        normal_content,
+        true,
+    );
+    let verbatim_id =
+        crate::id_minting::derive_content_id(CONTESTED_ID, &verbatim.losing_blob_oid, |c| {
+            c == CONTESTED_ID
+        });
+    let verbatim_path = super::doogat_path(&verbatim_id.0, None, false);
+    let normal_id =
+        crate::id_minting::derive_content_id("20260301130000", &normal.losing_blob_oid, |c| {
+            c == CONTESTED_ID || c == verbatim_id.0
+        });
+    let normal_path = super::doogat_path(&normal_id.0, None, false);
+    let normal_expected = crate::parser::rewrite_id_field(normal_content, &normal_id.0).unwrap();
+    let head_before = repo_b.head_oid().unwrap();
+
+    let outcome = repo_b
+        .commit_merge(
+            &[(CONTESTED_PATH, WINNER)],
+            &[],
+            &[verbatim, normal],
+            "merge",
+            &theirs_oid,
+        )
+        .unwrap();
+
+    let merge_commit = repo_b.head_commit().unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(merge_commit.id().to_string(), outcome.commit.0);
+    assert!(
+        merge_commit
+            .parents()
+            .any(|p| p.id().to_string() == head_before.0),
+        "both losers must land in one commit built directly on the pre-merge HEAD"
+    );
+    assert_eq!(
+        head_blob_bytes(&repo_b, &verbatim_path).unwrap(),
+        FRONTMATTERLESS_LOSER.as_bytes()
+    );
+    assert_eq!(
+        head_blob_bytes(&repo_b, &normal_path).unwrap(),
+        normal_expected.as_bytes()
+    );
+    assert_eq!(
+        outcome.losers_kept_verbatim,
+        vec![crate::types::CollisionLoserKeptVerbatim {
+            old_id: CONTESTED_ID.to_string(),
+            old_path: CONTESTED_PATH.to_string(),
+            new_path: verbatim_path,
+        }]
+    );
+}

+/// The same frontmatter-less collision resolved from either side (loser is
+/// ours and theirs wins, or loser is theirs and ours wins) must derive the
+/// same destination and land the same bytes there.
+#[test]
+fn frontmatterless_loser_derives_the_same_path_in_either_merge_direction() {
+    let resolve = |ours: &str, theirs: &str, theirs_won: bool| {
+        let (_a, _b, _bare, repo_b, theirs_oid) = conflicted_add_add(ours, theirs);
+        let loser = loser_at(
+            &repo_b,
+            CONTESTED_ID,
+            CONTESTED_PATH,
+            FRONTMATTERLESS_LOSER,
+            theirs_won,
+        );
+        let outcome = repo_b
+            .commit_merge(
+                &[(CONTESTED_PATH, WINNER)],
+                &[],
+                &[loser],
+                "merge",
+                &theirs_oid,
+            )
+            .unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(outcome.losers_kept_verbatim.len(), 1);
+        let new_path = outcome.losers_kept_verbatim[0].new_path.clone();
+        let bytes = head_blob_bytes(&repo_b, &new_path).unwrap();
+        (new_path, bytes)
+    };
+
+    let loser_ours = resolve(FRONTMATTERLESS_LOSER, WINNER, true);
+    let loser_theirs = resolve(WINNER, FRONTMATTERLESS_LOSER, false);
+
+    assert_eq!(
+        loser_ours.0, loser_theirs.0,
+        "destination must not depend on direction"
+    );
+    assert_eq!(loser_ours.1, FRONTMATTERLESS_LOSER.as_bytes());
+    assert_eq!(loser_theirs.1, FRONTMATTERLESS_LOSER.as_bytes());
+}

+/// closing `---`), judged by the same rule `split_zones` uses. Says nothing
+/// about whether the YAML inside the block is valid.
+pub(crate) fn has_frontmatter(content: &str) -> bool {
+    let lines: Vec<&str> = content.lines().collect();
+    find_frontmatter(&lines).is_ok()
+}

 succeeded in 0ms:
use git2::Oid;

use super::{validate_path, GitRepo};
use crate::error::{DoogatError, Result};
use crate::types::{
    CollisionLoserKeptVerbatim, CommitHash, ConflictFile, MergeCommitOutcome, MergeResult,
};

impl GitRepo {
    /// Create a merge commit with two parents from a true three-way merge tree,
    /// overlaying the CRDT-resolved blobs onto the conflict entries and folding
    /// each collision loser's reassigned content into the SAME commit (PRD 00167
    /// — never a second commit for losers).
    #[cfg_attr(feature = "profiling", tracing::instrument(skip_all))]
    pub fn commit_merge(
        &self,
        files: &[(&str, &str)],
        binary: &[(&str, &str)], // (path, winning-blob-OID)
        losers: &[crate::types::CollisionLoser],
        message: &str,
        theirs: &CommitHash,
    ) -> Result<MergeCommitOutcome> {
        let outcome = self.with_write_lock(|| {
            for (rel_path, _) in files {
                validate_path(&self.path, rel_path)?;
            }
            for (rel_path, _) in binary {
                validate_path(&self.path, rel_path)?;
            }

            let our_commit = self
                .head_commit()
                .ok_or_else(|| DoogatError::Git("repo has no initial commit".into()))?;
            let theirs_oid = Oid::from_str(&theirs.0)?;
            let their_commit = self.repo.find_commit(theirs_oid)?;

            // Reuse the clean path's construction: libgit2 computes the true
            // three-way merge tree (ours-only edits kept, theirs-only kept,
            // both-edited auto-mergeable files line-merged, theirs-deletions
            // absent, ours-creates present). Only the conflict entries need
            // our CRDT-resolved blobs overlaid.
            let mut merge_index = self.repo.merge_commits(&our_commit, &their_commit, None)?;

            // DIVERGENCE GUARD (fail-loud, in-scope per PRD "conflict-set divergence
            // handling"). The re-run merge's conflict set MUST equal the set
            // `sync_manager` resolved (`files` + `binary`). If a concurrent writer
            // moved HEAD in the resolve→commit window, the re-run set differs — a
            // path auto-resolved (no longer a conflict) or a new path conflicts —
            // and our resolved blobs are stale. Checking `has_conflicts()` AFTER the
            // overlay is NOT sufficient: `conflict_remove` returns Ok (not NotFound)
            // for an auto-resolved stage-0 path, so the overlay would silently
            // overwrite libgit2's fresh result and `has_conflicts()` would be false.
            // Snapshot the conflict paths BEFORE the overlay clears them and compare.
            // Because the whole tree is built in-memory (no worktree write until the
            // post-commit checkout), an abort HERE mutates nothing on disk — the
            // binary winners are overlaid by OID, not pre-written (fixes the stale-
            // binary-on-abort window a pre-write would leave).
            let rerun_conflicts = self.collect_conflict_paths(&merge_index)?;
            let resolved: std::collections::HashSet<&str> = files
                .iter()
                .map(|(p, _)| *p)
                .chain(binary.iter().map(|(p, _)| *p))
                .collect();
            if rerun_conflicts.len() != resolved.len()
                || !rerun_conflicts.iter().all(|p| resolved.contains(p.as_str()))
            {
                return Err(DoogatError::Conflict(
                    "merge conflict set changed since resolution (HEAD moved during the \
                     resolve→commit window); retry sync"
                        .into(),
                ));
            }

            self.overlay_resolved_blobs(&mut merge_index, files, binary)?;
            let losers_kept_verbatim =
                self.fold_losers_into_index(&mut merge_index, losers, &our_commit, &their_commit)?;

            // `write_tree_to` additionally refuses any not-fully-merged index — a
            // second backstop, though the equality guard above already guarantees the
            // overlay clears every conflict.
            let tree_oid = merge_index.write_tree_to(&self.repo)?;
            let tree = self.repo.find_tree(tree_oid)?;
            if let Some(theirs_hlc) = crate::hlc::extract_hlc(their_commit.message().unwrap_or("")) {
                self.hlc_clock.recv(&theirs_hlc);
            }
            let oid = self.create_commit(message, &tree, &[&our_commit, &their_commit])?;
            // Sync the working tree to the committed merge tree — symmetric with
            // `perform_normal_merge`. This is what removes theirs-deleted files from
            // the worktree and materializes line-merged and resolved content,
            // replacing the old manual `write_resolved_files`.
            self.repo
                .checkout_head(Some(git2::build::CheckoutBuilder::new().force()))?;
            Ok(MergeCommitOutcome {
                commit: CommitHash(oid.to_string()),
                losers_kept_verbatim,
            })
        })?;
        self.write_commit_graph();
        Ok(outcome)
    }

    /// Overlay CRDT-resolved blobs onto a `merge_commits` index's conflict entries.
    /// Text blobs are created from `files` (path, resolved-content); binary-reference
    /// blobs are overlaid by their already-existing winning OID (`binary`: path,
    /// oid-string) — no worktree read/write, so the whole tree is built in memory and
    /// an abort before commit mutates nothing on disk. After this, every path the
    /// resolver produced is a single stage-0 entry; the post-commit `checkout_head`
    /// materializes both text and binary content into the worktree.
    fn overlay_resolved_blobs(
        &self,
        index: &mut git2::Index,
        files: &[(&str, &str)],
        binary: &[(&str, &str)],
    ) -> Result<()> {
        for (path, content) in files {
            let oid = self.repo.blob(content.as_bytes())?;
            Self::resolve_index_entry(index, path, oid)?;
        }
        for (path, oid_str) in binary {
            let oid = git2::Oid::from_str(oid_str)?;
            Self::resolve_index_entry(index, path, oid)?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Fold each collision loser's reassigned content (new deterministic ID,
    /// rewritten frontmatter, rewritten inbound links) into `merge_index`, inside
    /// the same merge commit as the winner. Losers are processed sequentially so
    /// each loser's id derivation sees every prior loser's already-taken id —
    /// this is what makes two losers in the same call land at distinct paths
    /// even if their computed candidates are close together.
    ///
    /// Two-branch rule, decided per loser in `fold_one_loser`:
    /// - A loser with NO frontmatter block has no id field to rewrite, so its
    ///   bytes land verbatim at the derived path and it is returned in the
    ///   kept-verbatim list the caller reports.
    /// - A loser WITH a frontmatter block is id-rewritten. If that rewrite
    ///   fails (e.g. the YAML inside the block is invalid), the loser aborts
    ///   this call via `?`, which aborts `commit_merge` before its single
    ///   `create_commit` — nothing lands, not the winner, not an earlier loser
    ///   in the same batch. Per-loser degradation is deliberately not offered:
    ///   landing the winner while silently dropping a loser is exactly the
    ///   half-resolved data-loss shape PRD 00167 removed. The repo's "one bad
    ///   file never fails the batch" P0 invariant scopes to read/list/index
    ///   paths, not to this git write path.
    fn fold_losers_into_index(
        &self,
        merge_index: &mut git2::Index,
        losers: &[crate::types::CollisionLoser],
        our_commit: &git2::Commit,
        their_commit: &git2::Commit,
    ) -> Result<Vec<CollisionLoserKeptVerbatim>> {
        let mut taken_ids = Self::collect_taken_ids(merge_index);
        let mut kept_verbatim = Vec::new();
        for loser in losers {
            let winner_commit = if loser.theirs_won {
                their_commit
            } else {
                our_commit
            };
            if let Some(kept) =
                self.fold_one_loser(merge_index, loser, winner_commit, &mut taken_ids)?
            {
                kept_verbatim.push(kept);
            }
        }
        Ok(kept_verbatim)
    }

    /// Snapshot every doogat id already present anywhere under `ddb/` -- other
    /// type folders and `ddb/_typedef/` included, neither of which
    /// `doogat_path` can even address -- once, up front. An id is taken
    /// repo-wide, not just at the loser's own flat/type-folder path. Kept
    /// current as each loser lands (mirrors `id_minting::existence_oracle`'s
    /// snapshot-then-answer idiom, but against the in-memory merge tree
    /// instead of HEAD) so a later loser in the same batch still sees an
    /// earlier loser's just-assigned id as taken.
    fn collect_taken_ids(merge_index: &git2::Index) -> std::collections::HashSet<String> {
        merge_index
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|entry| {
                let path = String::from_utf8(entry.path.clone()).ok()?;
                if !path.starts_with("ddb/") || !path.ends_with(".md") {
                    return None;
                }
                crate::parser::extract_id_from_path(&path)
            })
            .collect()
    }

    /// Fold one collision loser's reassigned content (new deterministic ID,
    /// rewritten frontmatter, rewritten inbound links) into `merge_index`.
    /// `taken_ids` is threaded through by mutable reference and updated with
    /// the newly derived id before returning, so the next loser processed in
    /// the same `fold_losers_into_index` call sees it as taken. Returns the
    /// kept-verbatim record when the loser had no frontmatter block and its
    /// bytes landed unchanged; `None` when it was id-rewritten.
    fn fold_one_loser(
        &self,
        merge_index: &mut git2::Index,
        loser: &crate::types::CollisionLoser,
        winner_commit: &git2::Commit,
        taken_ids: &mut std::collections::HashSet<String>,
    ) -> Result<Option<CollisionLoserKeptVerbatim>> {
        let exists = |candidate: &str| -> bool { taken_ids.contains(candidate) };
        let new_id =
            crate::id_minting::derive_content_id(&loser.old_id, &loser.losing_blob_oid, exists);
        taken_ids.insert(new_id.0.clone());
        let new_path =
            crate::git_ops::doogat_path(&new_id.0, loser.type_name.as_deref(), loser.folder);
        let (new_content, kept_verbatim) = if crate::parser::has_frontmatter(&loser.content) {
            let rewritten =
                crate::parser::rewrite_id_field(&loser.content, &new_id.0).map_err(|e| {
                    DoogatError::Conflict(format!(
                        "collision loser at {} (old id {}) could not be rewritten: {e}",
                        loser.old_path, loser.old_id
                    ))
                })?;
            (rewritten, None)
        } else {
            let kept = CollisionLoserKeptVerbatim {
                old_id: loser.old_id.clone(),
                old_path: loser.old_path.clone(),
                new_path: new_path.clone(),
            };
            (loser.content.clone(), Some(kept))
        };

        tracing::warn!(
            old_id = %loser.old_id,
            new_id = %new_id.0,
            old_path = %loser.old_path,
            new_path = %new_path,
            "collision resolved: doogat ID reassigned"
        );

        let old_path_no_ext = loser
            .old_path
            .strip_suffix(".md")
            .unwrap_or(&loser.old_path);
        let new_path_no_ext = new_path.strip_suffix(".md").unwrap_or(&new_path);

        let rewritten_links = self.scan_and_rewrite_links_in_index(
            merge_index,
            winner_commit,
            &loser.old_id,
            old_path_no_ext,
            &new_id.0,
            new_path_no_ext,
        )?;

        let oid = self.repo.blob(new_content.as_bytes())?;
        Self::resolve_index_entry(merge_index, &new_path, oid)?;

        for (path, content) in rewritten_links {
            let oid = self.repo.blob(content.as_bytes())?;
            Self::resolve_index_entry(merge_index, &path, oid)?;
        }
        Ok(kept_verbatim)
    }

    /// Scan every `ddb/*.md` entry in `index` for inline references to `old_id`
    /// (frontmatter id, ID-form links, or path-form links), skipping any
    /// reference that also appears in `winner_commit`'s tree at the same path —
    /// that reference was written against the doogat that KEPT `old_id`, not the
    /// loser, so it must not be rewritten. `winner_commit` is whichever side
    /// `lww_pick` chose (`CollisionLoser::theirs_won`); since PRD 00166 ours can
    /// win too, keying this on theirs would corrupt the winner's own backlinks.
    /// Does not write to `index`; returns `(path, rewritten_content)` pairs for
    /// `fold_one_loser` to overlay.
    fn scan_and_rewrite_links_in_index(
        &self,
        index: &git2::Index,
        winner_commit: &git2::Commit,
        old_id: &str,
        old_path_no_ext: &str,
        new_id: &str,
        new_path_no_ext: &str,
    ) -> Result<Vec<(String, String)>> {
        let mut rewritten = Vec::new();
        for entry in index.iter() {
            let Ok(entry_path) = String::from_utf8(entry.path.clone()) else {
                continue;
            };
            if !entry_path.starts_with("ddb/") || !entry_path.ends_with(".md") {
                continue;
            }
            let blob = self.repo.find_blob(entry.id)?;
            let content = match std::str::from_utf8(blob.content()) {
                Ok(c) => c,
                Err(e) => {
                    tracing::warn!(
                        path = %entry_path,
                        error = %e,
                        "skipping non-UTF-8 blob in collision link-rewrite scan"
                    );
                    continue;
                }
            };
            if !content.contains(old_id) {
                continue;
            }

            if self.winner_tree_mentions_id(winner_commit, &entry_path, old_id)? {
                continue;
            }

            let by_id = crate::parser::rewrite_links(content, old_id, new_id);
            let rewritten_content =
                crate::parser::rewrite_links(&by_id, old_path_no_ext, new_path_no_ext);
            if rewritten_content != content {
                rewritten.push((entry_path, rewritten_content));
            }
        }
        Ok(rewritten)
    }

    /// True when `winner_commit`'s tree holds `entry_path` and that blob also
    /// mentions `old_id`: the reference was written against the doogat that KEPT
    /// `old_id`, so the loser's rewrite must leave it alone. A path absent from
    /// the winner's tree is not a winner reference, hence `false`.
    fn winner_tree_mentions_id(
        &self,
        winner_commit: &git2::Commit,
        entry_path: &str,
        old_id: &str,
    ) -> Result<bool> {
        let Ok(winner_entry) = winner_commit
            .tree()?
            .get_path(std::path::Path::new(entry_path))
        else {
            return Ok(false);
        };
        let winner_blob = self.repo.find_blob(winner_entry.id())?;
        Ok(String::from_utf8_lossy(winner_blob.content()).contains(old_id))
    }

    /// Replace any conflict at `path` with a single stage-0 entry for `oid`.
    /// `conflict_remove` clears the ancestor/ours/theirs stages
    /// (`git_index_conflict_remove` — keeps stage 0, drops stages 1-3);
    /// a path with no conflict returns `ErrorCode::NotFound`, which is fine — the
    /// following `add` installs (or overwrites) the stage-0 entry either way.
    ///
    /// NOTE: `Index::add` alone does NOT clear conflict stages — for a stage-0
    /// insert on a conflicted path, `index_insert` finds no stage-0 existing entry
    /// and appends stage 0 *alongside* stages 1-3, so `write_tree_to` would still
    /// refuse the index. The explicit `conflict_remove` is required.
    fn resolve_index_entry(index: &mut git2::Index, path: &str, oid: git2::Oid) -> Result<()> {
        match index.conflict_remove(std::path::Path::new(path)) {
            Ok(()) => {}
            Err(e) if e.code() == git2::ErrorCode::NotFound => {}
            Err(e) => return Err(e.into()),
        }
        index.add(&Self::stage0_entry(path, oid))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// The set of paths that still carry conflict entries in `index`. The path is
    /// taken from the ours side, falling back to theirs (a modify/delete conflict
    /// has one side absent) — matching `extract_single_conflict`'s path logic. Used
    /// by the divergence guard to compare the re-run merge's conflicts against the
    /// set `sync_manager` resolved.
    fn collect_conflict_paths(
        &self,
        index: &git2::Index,
    ) -> Result<std::collections::HashSet<String>> {
        let mut paths = std::collections::HashSet::new();
        for conflict in index.conflicts()? {
            let conflict = conflict?;
            if let Some(p) = conflict
                .our
                .as_ref()
                .or(conflict.their.as_ref())
                .and_then(|e| String::from_utf8(e.path.clone()).ok())
            {
                paths.insert(p);
            }
        }
        Ok(paths)
    }

    /// A stage-0 `IndexEntry` for a regular file blob at `path`. Stat fields are
    /// zeroed; only `id`, `mode`, and `path` affect the written tree.
    fn stage0_entry(path: &str, oid: git2::Oid) -> git2::IndexEntry {
        git2::IndexEntry {
            ctime: git2::IndexTime::new(0, 0),
            mtime: git2::IndexTime::new(0, 0),
            dev: 0,
            ino: 0,
            mode: 0o100644, // GIT_FILEMODE_BLOB
            uid: 0,
            gid: 0,
            file_size: 0,
            id: oid,
            flags: 0, // stage 0; `Index::add` recomputes the path-length namemask bits
            flags_extended: 0,
            path: path.as_bytes().to_vec(),
        }
    }

    /// Perform a normal (non-fast-forward) merge and return the result.
    fn perform_normal_merge(
        &self,
        remote: &str,
        branch: &str,
        annotated: &git2::AnnotatedCommit,
        allow_unrelated: bool,
    ) -> Result<MergeResult> {
        let their_commit = self.repo.find_commit(annotated.id())?;
        let our_commit = self
            .head_commit()
            .ok_or_else(|| DoogatError::Parse("no HEAD".into()))?;
        // The unrelated-histories guard is skipped only when the caller
        // explicitly opts in via `merge_remote_allowing_unrelated` (bundle
        // import: a fresh repo importing an established bundle has no common
        // ancestor by design). Ordinary `merge_remote` always enforces it: a
        // sync merge with no common ancestor is almost always a
        // misconfigured remote, and should keep failing loudly instead of
        // silently unioning two unrelated trees.
        if !allow_unrelated {
            self.repo.merge_base(our_commit.id(), their_commit.id())?;
        }

        let mut merge_index = self.repo.merge_commits(&our_commit, &their_commit, None)?;

        if merge_index.has_conflicts() {
            let conflicts = self.extract_conflicts(&merge_index, &our_commit, &their_commit)?;
            self.repo.cleanup_state()?;
            return Ok(MergeResult::Conflicts(
                conflicts,
                CommitHash(their_commit.id().to_string()),
            ));
        }

        let tree_oid = merge_index.write_tree_to(&self.repo)?;
        let tree = self.repo.find_tree(tree_oid)?;
        if let Some(theirs_hlc) = crate::hlc::extract_hlc(their_commit.message().unwrap_or("")) {
            self.hlc_clock.recv(&theirs_hlc);
        }
        let oid =
            self.create_commit(&format!("merge {remote}/{branch}"), &tree, &[&our_commit, &their_commit])?;
        self.repo
            .checkout_head(Some(git2::build::CheckoutBuilder::new().force()))?;
        Ok(MergeResult::Clean(CommitHash(oid.to_string())))
    }

    /// Merge a fetched remote branch, returning the merge result. Always
    /// enforces the unrelated-histories guard.
    #[cfg_attr(feature = "profiling", tracing::instrument(skip_all))]
    pub fn merge_remote(&self, remote: &str, branch: &str) -> Result<MergeResult> {
        self.merge_remote_with_policy(remote, branch, false)
    }

    /// Merge a fetched remote branch whose history is expected to share no
    /// common ancestor with the local one (bundle import). Identical to
    /// `merge_remote` except that the unrelated-histories guard is not applied.
    #[cfg_attr(feature = "profiling", tracing::instrument(skip_all))]
    pub fn merge_remote_allowing_unrelated(
        &self,
        remote: &str,
        branch: &str,
    ) -> Result<MergeResult> {
        self.merge_remote_with_policy(remote, branch, true)
    }

    /// Shared implementation behind `merge_remote` and
    /// `merge_remote_allowing_unrelated`; `allow_unrelated` is the only
    /// difference in behavior between the two.
    fn merge_remote_with_policy(
        &self,
        remote: &str,
        branch: &str,
        allow_unrelated: bool,
    ) -> Result<MergeResult> {
        let result = self.with_write_lock(|| {
            let fetch_head_ref = format!("refs/remotes/{remote}/{branch}");
            let reference = self
                .repo
                .find_reference(&fetch_head_ref)
                .map_err(|_| DoogatError::NotFound(fetch_head_ref.clone()))?;
            let annotated = self.repo.reference_to_annotated_commit(&reference)?;

            let (analysis, _pref) = self.repo.merge_analysis(&[&annotated])?;

            if analysis.is_up_to_date() {
                return Ok(MergeResult::AlreadyUpToDate);
            }

            if analysis.is_fast_forward() {
                let target_oid = annotated.id();
                let mut reference = self
                    .repo
                    .find_reference("refs/heads/master")
                    .or_else(|_| self.repo.find_reference("HEAD"))?;
                reference.set_target(target_oid, "fast-forward")?;
                self.repo.set_head("refs/heads/master")?;
                self.repo
                    .checkout_head(Some(git2::build::CheckoutBuilder::new().force()))?;
                if let Ok(their_commit) = self.repo.find_commit(target_oid) {
                    if let Some(theirs_hlc) =
                        crate::hlc::extract_hlc(their_commit.message().unwrap_or(""))
                    {
                        self.hlc_clock.recv(&theirs_hlc);
                    }
                }
                return Ok(MergeResult::FastForward(CommitHash(target_oid.to_string())));
            }

            self.perform_normal_merge(remote, branch, &annotated, allow_unrelated)
        })?;
        if matches!(result, MergeResult::FastForward(_) | MergeResult::Clean(_)) {
            self.write_commit_graph();
        }
        Ok(result)
    }

    /// Read blob content as a string, or return an empty string if the entry is None.
    fn read_blob_content(&self, entry: Option<&git2::IndexEntry>) -> Result<String> {
        match entry {
            Some(e) => {
                let blob = self.repo.find_blob(e.id)?;
                Ok(String::from_utf8_lossy(blob.content()).to_string())
            }
            None => Ok(String::new()),
        }
    }

    /// Extract a single conflict entry into a `ConflictFile`.
    fn extract_single_conflict(
        &self,
        conflict: &git2::IndexConflict,
        ours_commit: &git2::Commit,
        theirs_commit: &git2::Commit,
    ) -> Result<ConflictFile> {
        let path = conflict
            .our
            .as_ref()
            .or(conflict.their.as_ref())
            .and_then(|e| String::from_utf8(e.path.clone()).ok())
            .unwrap_or_default();

        let ancestor = match conflict.ancestor {
            Some(ref entry) => {
                let blob = self.repo.find_blob(entry.id)?;
                Some(String::from_utf8_lossy(blob.content()).to_string())
            }
            None => None,
        };
        let ours = self.read_blob_content(conflict.our.as_ref())?;
        let theirs = self.read_blob_content(conflict.their.as_ref())?;

        let ours_blob_oid = conflict.our.as_ref().map(|e| e.id.to_string());
        let theirs_blob_oid = conflict.their.as_ref().map(|e| e.id.to_string());

        let ours_hlc = self.find_hlc_for_path(ours_commit, &path);
        let theirs_hlc = self.find_hlc_for_path(theirs_commit, &path);

        Ok(ConflictFile {
            path,
            ancestor,
            ours,
            theirs,
            ours_hlc,
            theirs_hlc,
            ours_blob_oid,
            theirs_blob_oid,
        })
    }

    fn extract_conflicts(
        &self,
        index: &git2::Index,
        ours_commit: &git2::Commit,
        theirs_commit: &git2::Commit,
    ) -> Result<Vec<ConflictFile>> {
        let mut conflicts = Vec::new();
        for conflict in index.conflicts()? {
            let conflict = conflict?;
            conflicts.push(self.extract_single_conflict(&conflict, ours_commit, theirs_commit)?);
        }
        Ok(conflicts)
    }
}
